## S-DU PAO-LO SIA PEH KO-LONG-TO NYING DI-IH-FONG SHÜ-SING. 使徒<u>保罗</u>写拨<u>哥林多</u>人第一封书信

### 1K. I.

<ruby><rb>奉</rb><rt>VONG</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>召来</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所提尼</rb><rt>Sô-di-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>*mông-mông*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召来</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>圣徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>；（<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt></ruby>。）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>赐落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>时常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>富足</rb><rt>fu-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>口才</rb><rt>kʽeo-dzæ</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>知识</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt></ruby>；<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>靠实了</rb><rt>kʽao-jih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>缺少</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>恩赐</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>底</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>信实个</rb><rt>sing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>相同</rb><rt>siang-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做样</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>做样</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>合而为一</rb><rt>ʽeh-r-we-ih</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革来</rb><rt>Keh-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋里</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话尔拉</rb><rt>wô-ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；」「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚波罗</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；」「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>矶法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；」「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>分开了</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ-de</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保罗</u>个</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革利士布</rb><rt>Keh-li-z-pu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦犹</rb><rt>Kyüô-yiu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>；<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>呒</rb><rt>m</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>记弗起</rb><rt>kyi-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>*tsʽa*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>落空</rb><rt>lôh-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十字架个</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愚笨个</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>得救</rb><rt>teh-kyiu</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>废掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>见识</rb><rt>kyin-sih</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>见识</rb><rt>kyin-sih</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>辩论</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>愚笨</rb><rt>nyü-beng</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>终于</rb><rt>*cong-ü*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>识得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>愚笨</rb><rt>nyü-beng</rt><rb>传教个</rb><rt>djün-kyiao-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>刻忖个</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愚笨个</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>；<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召来</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>愚笨</rb><rt>nyü-beng</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>拣着</rb><rt>kæn-djôh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>卑贱个</rb><rt>pe-zin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>轻慢个</rb><rt>kyʽing-mæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拣着</rb><rt>*kæn-djôh*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>废掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>奥妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>*yi-zin*</rt><rb>隐藏的个</rb><rt>ing-dzông-tih-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>*tsʽong-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>万代</rb><rt>væn-dæ</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>时世个</rb><rt>z-shü-go</rt><rb>官长</rb><rt>kwun-tsiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>识得个</rb><rt>sih-teh-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>识得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt><rb>钉煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>荣华个</rb><rt>yüong-wô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>深奥个</rb><rt>sing-ao-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>接受个</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>教个</rb><rt>kao-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>教个</rb><rt>kao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>灵勒紧个</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>灵勒紧个</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>感化</rb><rt>ken-hwô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>灵个</rb><rt>Ling-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愚笨个</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>识得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>灵勒紧个</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>查察<sup> </sup>个</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」 <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Dzô-tsʽah, *ʽôh* *wô*, bin-cʽih. 查察，或话，辨出。（原注）</sup>

### 1K. III.

<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>灵勒紧个</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt><rb>饭</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>加弗起</rb><rt>kô-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>加弗起</rb><rt>kô-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>妒忌</rb><rt>tu-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分开个</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>款式</rb><rt>kwʽun-shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保罗</u>个</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚波罗</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo-go</rt></ruby>；」<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>亚波罗</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>种个</rb><rt>cong-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚波罗</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>浇个</rb><rt>kyiao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大起来</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>种个</rb><rt>cong-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒要紧</rb><rt>m-iao-kying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>浇个</rb><rt>kyiao-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒要紧</rb><rt>m-iao-kying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大起来</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>要紧</rb><rt>*iao-kying*</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>种个</rb><rt>cong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浇个</rb><rt>kyiao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>劳碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>帮手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>田亩</rb><rt>din-meo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好像</rb><rt>hao-ziang</rt><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>作头</rb><rt>tsôh-deo</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>放好</rb><rt>fông-hao</rt><rb>墙脚</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>去造上去</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>起上去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-zông-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>放间个</rb><rt>fông-kæn-go</rt><rb>墙脚</rb><rt>*ziang-kyiah*</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>除此之外</rb><rt>djü-tsʽ-ts-nga</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>墙脚</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>宝石</rb><rt>pao-zah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>*ʽôh-tsia*</rt><rb>树木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稻草</rb><rt>dao-tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>芦竿</rb><rt>lu-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去造</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>墙脚个</rb><rt>ziang-kyiah-go</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>；<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>各人个</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>看出</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>表</rb><rt>piao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>猛</rb><rt>*mang*</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>试出</rb><rt>s-cʽih</rt><rb>各人个</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>起上去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-zông-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>除掉</rb><rt>djü-diao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>*kong-din*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>缝里</rb><rt>vong-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>救出个</rb><rt>*kyiu-cʽih-go*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>坏掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>坏掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>就是个</rb><rt>ziu-z-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Wæ-diao; *ʽôh* *wô*, hwe-diao. 坏掉；或话，毁掉。（原注）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Kôh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>骗</rb><rt>pʽin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>愚笨个</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>实惠</rb><rt>*jih-we*</rt><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愚笨个</rb><rt>nyü-beng-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>诈计</rb><rt>tsô-kyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>*ih-kyü*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>忖头</rb><rt>tsʽeng-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>虚妄</rb><rt>hyü-vông</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>夸口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚波罗</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>矶法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>世界</rb><rt>shü-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt></ruby>；<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt></ruby>；<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. IV.

<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>手下人</rb><rt>siu-ʽô-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>奥妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>账房</rb><rt>tsiang-vông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>账房</rb><rt>tsiang-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>*dzô-tsʽah*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>毛病</rb><rt>*mao-bing*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>未必</rb><rt>vi-pih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>囥的个</rb><rt>kʽông-tih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>㪗出来</rb><rt>tʽeo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>*cong-nying*</rt><rb>心里个</rb><rt>sing-li-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>门份个</rb><rt>*meng-veng-go*</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, be nying-go nyih-ts *dzô-tsʽah*. 本文字眼，被人个日子查察。（原注）</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>藉</rb><rt>tsia</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚波罗</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>印子</rb><rt>ing-ts</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>印头</rb><rt>ing-deo</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>过如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>圣书里</rb><rt>*Sing-shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>荡</rb><rt>dông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>自尊自大</rb><rt>zi-tseng-zi-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>弗起</rb><rt>feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>各样</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>夸口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>？

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>吃饱了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>富足了</rb><rt>fu-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>*de*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>巴弗能够</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>*jih-dzæ*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>摆出</rb><rt>pa-cʽih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>卑贱个</rb><rt>pe-zin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>*væn-nying*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>可比</rb><rt>*kʽo-pi*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>看头</rb><rt>kʽen-deo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愚笨</rb><rt>nyü-beng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>刚强个</rb><rt>kông-gyiang-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>体面</rb><rt>tʽi-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>倒霉个</rb><rt>tao-me-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>Dzih-tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>出膊</rb><rt>cʽih-poh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>挨打</rb><rt>ngæ-tang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一定个</rb><rt>ih-ding-go</rt><rb>庉处</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>劳劳碌碌</rb><rt>lao-lao-loh-loh</rt><rb>亲手</rb><rt>tsʽing-siu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>𧮙骂</rb><rt>zôh-mô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>忍气吞声</rb><rt>jing-kyʽi-tʽeng-sing</rt></ruby>；<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>谤毁</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>垃圾</rb><rt>leh-seh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>鏖糟个</rb><rt>ao-tsao-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>关照</rb><rt>kwæn-tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>值钿个</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>论万</rb><rt>leng-væn</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>学</rb><rt>ʽôh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>样</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>值钿个</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>做说</rb><rt>tso-shih</rt><rb>提醒</rb><rt>di-sing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到处</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>*Ng-lah-go*</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>*cong-nyiang*</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自尊自大</rb><rt>zi-tseng-zi-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恶像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯个</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>考究</rb><rt>kʽao-kyiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>自尊自大</rb><rt>zi-tseng-zi-do</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>考究</rb><rt>*kʽao-kyiu*</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>口才</rb><rt>kʽeo-dzæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>口才</rb><rt>kʽeo-dzæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>教方</rb><rt>kao-fông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>仁爱</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt><rb>温柔个</rb><rt>w̆eng-ziu-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？

### 1K. V.

<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>NYING-KÔ</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>淫乱</rb><rt>ying-lön</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>淫乱</rb><rt>ying-lön</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>提起过</rb><rt>di-kyʽi-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>甚至于</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>后母</rb><rt>ʽeo-meo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>自尊自大</rb><rt>zi-tseng-zi-do</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>痛哭</rb><rt>tʽong-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>亲身</rb><rt>tsʽing-sing</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> （<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聚集拢</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>*cong-nyiang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby> <sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>夸口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>一眼个</rb><rt>ih-ngæn-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>和团</rb><rt>ʽo-dön</rt><rb>发起</rb><rt>fah-kyʽi</rt><rb>酵</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>簇新</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sing</rt><rb>一团</rb><rt>ih-dön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒酵水</rb><rt>m-kao-shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>杀掉了</rb><rt>sah-diao-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>恶意</rb><rt>ôh-i</rt><rb>恶毒个</rb><rt>ôh-doh-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>清洁</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih</rt><rb>诚实个</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt><rb>无酵饼</rb><rt>vu-kao-ping</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, sah-diao tso tsi-veh. 或话，杀掉做祭物。（原注）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇封</rb><rt>keh-fong</rt><rb>信里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>凑队</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>奸淫个</rb><rt>kæn-ying-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>贪财个</rb><rt>tʽen-dzæ-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>索诈个</rb><rt>sôh-tsô-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>凑队</rb><rt>*tsʽeo-de*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>巧勒</rb><rt>kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>跳出</rb><rt>tʽiao-cʽih</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>贪</rb><rt>tʽen</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>𧮙骂个</rb><rt>zôh-mô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>吃醉个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>索诈</rb><rt>sôh-tsô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>凑队</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>饭</rb><rt>*væn*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>外头个</rb><rt>nga-deo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>里头个</rb><rt>li-deo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>外头个</rb><rt>nga-deo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>恶人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」 ### 1K. VI.

<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>NG-LAH-GO</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一起</rb><rt>ih-kyʽi</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣徒个</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>倒倒</rb><rt>tao-tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>霉</rb><rt>me</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>理处</rb><rt>li-cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>中央个</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>错了</rb><rt>tsʽo-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>何弗</rb><rt>ʽO-feh</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>何弗</rb><rt>ʽO-feh</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>吃亏</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kwʽe</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>*zi-go*</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吃亏</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kwʽe</rt></ruby>。

</sup><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>爱</rb><rt>æ</rt><rb>男风</rb><rt>nen-fong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>、<sup>10</sup> <sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>未必</rb><rt>vi-pih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>吃食</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>坏掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>肢体</rb><rt>ts-tʽi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>肢体</rb><rt>ts-tʽi</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>婊子个</rb><rt>piao-ts-go</rt><rb>肢体</rb><rt>ts-tʽi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>断断</rb><rt>Tön-tön</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>胶粘</rb><rt>kao-nyin</rt><rb>婊子</rb><rt>piao-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>*Sing-shü*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>胶粘</rb><rt>kao-nyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>犯个</rb><rt>væn-go</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>圣灵个</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>价钿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt><rb>买来个</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两样</rb><rt>*liang-yiang*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt></ruby>。
