### 1K. VII.

<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>KÔNG-TAO</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>Nen-nyüing</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>门份个</rb><rt>meng-veng-go</rt><rb>好意</rb><rt>hao-i</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>Lao-nyüing</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>男𭑸个</rb><rt>nen-nyüing-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>*cü-i*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>老𭑸个</rb><rt>lao-nyüing-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>*cü-i*</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>耽误</rb><rt>tæn-ngwu</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>暂时</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>相别</rb><rt>*siang-bih*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>专门</rb><rt>cün-meng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>祷告个</rb><rt>tao-kao-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>欲心</rb><rt>yüôh-sing</rt><rb>禁弗住</rb><rt>kying-feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>引诱</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>体谅</rb><rt>tʽi-liang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>*feh-jün*</rt><rb>巴弗能够</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>*sô*</rt><rb>得着个</rb><rt>*teh-djôh-go*</rt><rb>恩赐</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>欲心</rb><rt>yüôh-sing</rt><rb>禁弗住</rb><rt>kying-feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>*neng-s*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>婚配</rb><rt>hweng-pʽe</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>动</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>欲火</rb><rt>yüôh-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>婚配</rb><rt>hweng-pʽe</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>婚配个</rb><rt>hweng-pʽe-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>*feng-fu-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>Lao-nyüing</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>和拢</rb><rt>ʽo-long</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>余多个</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>*wô-go*</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>Tʽông-jün</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>住东</rb><rt>djü-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>住东</rb><rt>djü-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尊起了</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>尊起了</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尊起个</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>Tʽông-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>缚住了</rb><rt>bo-djü-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>和睦</rb><rt>ʽo-moh</rt><rb>做主</rb><rt>tso-cü</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>未可知</rb><rt>vi-kʽo-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>未可知</rb><rt>vi-kʽo-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, z dzæ-ü ʽo-moh. 或话，杀掉做祭物。（原注）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召来个</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>遮掩</rb><rt>tsô-in</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召来个</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒要紧</rb><rt>m-iao-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>呒要紧</rb><rt>m-iao-kying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>要紧</rb><rt>*iao-kying*</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Kôh-nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召来</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召来</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>弗妨得个</rb><rt>feh-fông-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>趁</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>机会</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>召来个</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>释放了</rb><rt>sih-fông-liao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召来个</rb><rt>dziao-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>价钿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt><rb>买来个</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召来</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>KÔNG-TAO</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>*ngô-go*</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>现在个</rb><rt>yin-dzæ-go</rt><rb>难头</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>结拢</rb><rt>kyih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>拆开</rb><rt>tsʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>结拢</rb><rt>kyih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>Jün-r</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>出嫁</rb><rt>cʽih-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>箇种介个</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>肉身个</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>难处</rb><rt>næn-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>不忍相</rb><rt>peh-jing-siang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kying-kyih. 或话，紧急。（原注）</sup>

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日脚</rb><rt>Nyih-kyiah</rt><rb>短了</rb><rt>tön-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>呒老𭑸</rb><rt>m-lao-nyüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>啼哭个</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>快活个</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>买进个</rb><rt>ma-tsing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>过于</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>样式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>担</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>心事</rb><rt>sing-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>M-neh</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>Lao-nyüing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>各样个</rb><rt>kôh-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>M-neh</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>清洁</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>马笼头</rb><rt>mô-long-deo</rt><rb>套</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>头里</rb><rt>deo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>*tso-nying*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>体面</rb><rt>tʽi-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>一成济路</rb><rt>ih-dzing-tsi-lu</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>错过</rb><rt>tsʽo-ko</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒体面</rb><rt>m-tʽi-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>巧勒</rb><rt>kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>婚配</rb><rt>hweng-pʽe</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>坚决个</rb><rt>kyin-kyüih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>歇弗来</rb><rt>hyih-feh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>定规</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>留住</rb><rt>liu-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嫁出</rb><rt>kô-cʽih</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>*nyiang-ts*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>嫁出</rb><rt>kô-cʽih</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>*nyiang-ts*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>缚住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>守东</rb><rt>siu-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. VIII.

<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>KÔNG-TAO</rt><rb>偶像个</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>知识</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>知识</rb><rt>Cü-sih</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>自尊自大</rb><rt>zi-tseng-zi-do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>仁爱</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>德气</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。）<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>偶像个</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>宇宙</rb><rt>yü-dziu</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>Ih-we</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>Se-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt></ruby>；（<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。）<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>归于</rb><rt>kwe-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>知识</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>觉着</rb><rt>kôh-djôh</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>*tong-si*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>偶像个</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>黵着</rb><rt>tæn-djôh</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>吃食</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby><sup> <ruby><rb>假如</rb><rt>Kyüô-jü</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>知识</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>偶像个</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang-go</rt><rb>庙里</rb><rt>miao-li</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>*se-tsih*</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>甚至于</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>偶像个</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>懦弱个</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>知识</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>！<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伤</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>坚固个</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. IX.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Dziu</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>*s-du*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>职份个</rb><rt>tsih-veng-go</rt><rb>印证</rb><rt>ing-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>喝个</rb><rt>hah-go</rt><rb>章程</rb><rt>công-dzing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>教会里个</rb><rt>*kyiao-we-li-go*</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>章程</rb><rt>công-dzing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走来走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>余多个</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>矶法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>独独</rb><rt>doh-doh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>章程</rb><rt>công-dzing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>当兵</rb><rt>tông-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>种</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>一园</rb><rt>ih-yün</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>羊奶</rb><rt>yiang-na</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>*tæn-tsih*</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>*i-s*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法里</rb><rt>lih-fah-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>碾谷个</rb><rt>Nyin-koh-go</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>套</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>原是</rb><rt>Nyün-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>耕种个</rb><rt>Kang-cong-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>耕种</rb><rt>kang-cong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>应该个</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>稻</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>应该个</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>扎开</rb><rt>tsah-kʽæ</rt><rb>灵勒紧</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>肉身个</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>别人</rb><rt>Bih-nying</rt><rb>尚且</rb><rt>jông-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>*do*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何况</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>Peh-ko</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>担当</rb><rt>tæn-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>圣殿个</rb><rt>sing-din-go</rt><rb>饭</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>侍值</rb><rt>z-dzih</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>祭坛上个</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-zông-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>*li-veh*</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>度日</rb><rt>du-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>箇番</rb><rt>keh-fæn</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>夸口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>虚话</rb><rt>hyü-*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>夸口</rb><rt>kwʽô-kʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>巧勒</rb><rt>kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>赏</rb><rt>sông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>账房个</rb><rt>tsiang-vông-go</rt><rb>缺分</rb><rt>kyʽüih-veng</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>赏</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*Ziu-z*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>费</rb><rt>fi</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>本来</rb><rt>*peng-læ*</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人家个</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>情愿</rb><rt>*dzing-nyün*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>众人个</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打伙</rb><rt>tang-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>名下个</rb><rt>ming-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打伙</rb><rt>tang-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>名下个</rb><rt>ming-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>名下个</rb><rt>ming-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>呒律法个</rb><rt>m-lih-fah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打伙</rb><rt>tang-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>呒律法</rb><rt>m-lih-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>呒律法个</rb><rt>m-lih-fah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>呒律法</rb><rt>m-lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。）<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>懦弱个</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打伙</rb><rt>tang-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>懦弱个</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>懦弱个</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>打伙</rb><rt>tang-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>终于</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>福音个</rb><rt>foh-ing-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>校场里</rb><rt>kao-dziang-li</rt><rb>赛</rb><rt>sæ</rt><rb>奔竞</rb><rt>peng-gying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>奔</rb><rt>peng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>赏</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>奔法</rb><rt>peng-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>赛</rb><rt>sæ</rt><rb>本事</rb><rt>peng-z</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>做忌</rb><rt>tso-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>*tso-fah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>坏掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>冠冕</rb><rt>kwun-min</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>*siang*</rt><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>坏掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>奔</rb><rt>peng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>呒数目账</rb><rt>m-su-moh-tsiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>拳头</rb><rt>gyün-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>空里</rb><rt>kʽong-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>压治</rb><rt>ah-djü</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尚恐怕</rb><rt>zông-kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>*foh-ing*</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>踢出</rb><rt>tʽih-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. X.

<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>云个</rb><rt>yüing-go</rt><rb>下头</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>经过</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt><rb>海个</rb><rt>hæ-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>云里</rb><rt>yüing-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>做样</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>灵勒紧个</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>做样</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>灵勒紧个</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>灵勒紧</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>流出来个</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>磐石</rb><rt>bun-zah</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大半</rb><rt>ih-do-pun</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>榜样</rb><rt>pông-yiang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>贪</rb><rt>tʽen</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>贪</rb><rt>tʽen</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt><rb>吃吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>嬉戏</rb><rt>hyi-hyi</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>二万</rb><rt>nyi-væn</rt><rb>三千</rb><rt>sæn-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>毒蛇</rb><rt>doh-dzô</rt><rb>丧命</rb><rt>sông-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>抄灭个</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>榜样</rb><rt>pông-yiang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>写落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>警戒</rb><rt>kying-kyiæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>末代</rb><rt>meh-dæ</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>临着个</rb><rt>ling-djôh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>未免</rb><rt>vi-min</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着个</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>平常</rb><rt>bing-djông</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>信实个</rb><rt>sing-jih-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>过于</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>迷惑个</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>出路</rb><rt>cʽih-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>挡勒住</rb><rt>tông-leh-djü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>亲爱个</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批评记</rb><rt>pʽi-bing-kyi</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>福气个</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>𤖼开个</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>分吃</rb><rt>feng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>*Wô*</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>偶像个</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>恶鬼</rb><rt>ôh-kyü</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>鬼个</rb><rt>kyü-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>酒筵</rb><rt>tsiu-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt><rb>鬼个</rb><rt>kyü-go</rt><rb>酒筵</rb><rt>tsiu-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>未必</rb><rt>vi-pih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>未必</rb><rt>vi-pih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>回</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>德气</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>M-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>*ih-cʽü*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>*siang*</rt><rb>别人个</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>相请</rb><rt>siang-tsʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>摆</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>伤</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>𢲔跌个</rb><rt>pæn-tih-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>众人个</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>*ih-cʽü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. XI.

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>*ngô-go*</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby><sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>男个</rb><rt>nen-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>女个</rb><rt>nyü-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>头里</rb><rt>deo-li</rt><rb>遮盖</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>呒体面</rb><rt>m-tʽi-min</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>头里</rb><rt>deo-li</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>遮盖</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>呒体面</rb><rt>m-tʽi-min</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>*deo-fah*</rt><rb>剃掉</rb><rt>tʽi-diao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>一色斯样个</rb><rt>ih-seh-s-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遮盖</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗如</rb><rt>feh-jü</rt><rb>剪掉</rb><rt>tsin-diao</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>*deo-fah*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>剪掉</rb><rt>tsin-diao</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>剃掉</rb><rt>tʽi-diao</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>*deo-fah*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>遮盖</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, li-tsih. 或话，礼节。（原注）</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>Nen</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应当</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>遮盖</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>男个</rb><rt>nen-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>造出来</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>造个</rb><rt>*zao-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>应当</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>头里</rb><rt>deo-li</rt><rb>带的</rb><rt>ta-tih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>隔开</rb><rt>kah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>隔开</rb><rt>kah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>生出来</rb><rt>*sang-cʽih-læ*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>究竟</rb><rt>kyiu-kying</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *doh*, *kyiao-we-li-go* s-tsʽa. 有得读，教会里个使差。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Sô* *wô* gyün-ping, *z* *deo-li* *ta-tih-go* *tong-si*, *tso* *voh* *nen* *go* *kyi-ʽao*. 所话权柄，是头里带的个东西，做服男个记号。（原注）</sup>

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批评相</rb><rt>pʽi-bing-siang</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遮盖</rb><rt>tsô-kæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>*deo*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>合理</rb><rt>ʽeh-li</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>合理</rb><rt>ʽeh-li</rt></ruby>？<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>生成个</rb><rt>sang-dzing-go</rt><rb>理性</rb><rt>li-sing</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>长长</rb><rt>dziang-dziang</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒体面</rb><rt>m-tʽi-min</rt></ruby>；<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>长长</rb><rt>dziang-dziang</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>遮盖个</rb><rt>tsô-kæ-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tông siu-pʽao. 或话，当手帕。（原注）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>*ng*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聚拢间</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>头一门</rb><rt>Deo-ih-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>教会里</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几分</rb><rt>kyi-feng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>结党</rb><rt>kyih-tông</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>试出了</rb><rt>s-cʽih-liao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>看出</rb><rt>kʽen-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tsang-leng. 或话，争论。（原注）</sup>

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚拢来</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>晚餐</rb><rt>væn-tsʽæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>抢抢</rb><rt>tsʽiang-tsʽiang</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>肚饥个</rb><rt>du-kyi-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃醉个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>看轻</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>称赞弗来</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, feh neng-keo. 有得翻，弗能够。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, gyüong-kwʽu-go. 或话，穷苦个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>传授</rb><rt>djün-ziu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>祝谢仔</rb><rt>coh-zia-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼开</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>𤖼开个</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>逾越</rb><rt>*Yü-yüih*</rt><rb>吃好</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-hao</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>*do-leh*</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>簇崭新个</rb><rt>tsʽoh-tsæn-sing-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>设立个</rb><rt>*shih-lih-go*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>表明</rb><rt>piao-ming</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>*tsæ*</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>周济</rb><rt>tsiu-tsi</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>*gyüong-nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>舍出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦煞</rb><rt>me-sah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>仁爱</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>无益</rb><rt>vu-ih</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. XIV.

<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>NG-LAH</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>上紧</rb><rt>zông-kying</rt><rb>追着</rb><rt>tse-djôh</rt><rb>仁爱</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>圣灵个</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling-go</rt><rb>恩赐</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>要紧</rb><rt>iao-kying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>Kông</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>德气</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>教会个</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>德气</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>翻译</rb><rt>fæn-yih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>德气</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假如</rb><rt>kyüô-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>知识</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>于</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>？<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>呒灵气</rb><rt>m-ling-kyʽi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>发出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>笛子</rb><rt>dih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>琵琶</rb><rt>bi-bô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>发出个</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-go</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>分别</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>吹出</rb><rt>cʽü-cʽih</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>腔</rb><rt>*kyʽiang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弹出</rb><rt>dæn-cʽih</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>调</rb><rt>*diao*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>号头</rb><rt>ʽao-deo</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>发出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>清通个</rb><rt>tsʽing-tʽong-go</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>舌头</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>讲出</rb><rt>kông-cʽih</rt><rb>明白个</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>空头个</rb><rt>kʽong-deo-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup>
