<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>懂</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>口音个</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>外国人</rb><rt>nga-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>外国人</rb><rt>nga-koh-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>恩赐</rb><rt>*eng-s*</rt><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>教会个</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>德气</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>原是</rb><rt>*nyün-z*</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒陶成</rb><rt>m-dao-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>明白个</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>歌唱</rb><rt>ko-tsʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>明白个</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>歌唱</rb><rt>ko-tsʽông</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>Feh-jün</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>祝赞</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白个</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>懂</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祝谢好</rb><rt>coh-zia-hao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>祝谢</rb><rt>coh-zia</rt><rb>原是</rb><rt>nyün-z</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>德气</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>聪明智慧</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-cü-we</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>聪明智慧</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-cü-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>成人长大</rb><rt>dzing-nying-tsiang-da</rt></ruby><sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>倘然</rb><rt>Tʽông-jün</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走进来</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>发狂</rb><rt>fah-gwông</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白个</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走进来</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>辨出</rb><rt>bin-cʽih</rt></ruby>；<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>隐藏的个</rb><rt>ing-dzông-tih-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>*i-s*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>显</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>KA-NI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>*ʽôh-tsia*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一首</rb><rt>ih-siu</rt><rb>诗</rb><rt>s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>*bih-cʽü*</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>翻译</rb><rt>fæn-yih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>德气</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只消</rb><rt>tsih-siao</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>多则</rb><rt>to-tseh</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>翻译</rb><rt>fæn-yih</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>翻译</rb><rt>fæn-yih</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>教会里</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>两三个</rb><rt>liang-sæn-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>辨别</rb><rt>bin-pih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>*gyi-go*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>*bông-pin*</rt><rb>坐东个</rb><rt>zo-tong-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>*kông-go*</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>歇口</rb><rt>hyih-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>学</rb><rt>ʽôh</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>乱七八糟</rb><rt>lön-tsʽih-pah-tsao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>和睦</rb><rt>ʽo-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣徒个</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tsʽao-lön. 或话，吵乱。（原注）</sup>

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>教会里</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>*nen*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>女𭑸</rb><rt>nyü-nyüing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>教会里</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惶恐相</rb><rt>wông-kʽong-siang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>单</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>灵勒紧个</rb><rt>ling-leh-kying-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>识得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>识得个</rb><rt>sih-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>识得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>土话</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>Yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>体面相</rb><rt>tʽi-min-siang</rt><rb>挨次序</rb><rt>a-tsʽ-jü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。

### 1K. XV.

<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>HYÜONG-DI</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立勒住</rb><rt>lih-leh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>；）<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>提醒</rb><rt>di-sing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>*foh-ing*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>空头</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, kyi-teh. 有得翻，记得。（原注）</sup>

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>传授</rb><rt>djün-ziu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>接受个</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>葬好仔</rb><rt>tsông-hao-ts</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>；<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>矶法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>五百多</rb><rt>ng-pah-to</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>困熟了</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大半</rb><rt>ih-do-pun</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>*yin-cʽih*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>现出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>月份</rb><rt>yüih-veng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>足</rb><rt>tsoh</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>亏了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>空头</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>劳碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>*kyiao*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>死人</rb><rt>Si-nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>空头</rb><rt>kʽong-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>坐罪了个</rb><rt>zo-ze-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>困熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>尝新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>咋挔</rb><rt>dza-yi</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>何用</rb><rt>ʽo-yüong</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>时刻</rb><rt>z-kʽeh</rt><rb>冒险</rb><rt>mao-hyin</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>高兴</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>高兴</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Dziu</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>讲来</rb><rt>kông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>斗过</rb><rt>teo-ko</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>落得</rb><rt>lôh-teh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>落得</rb><rt>lôh-teh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>骗</rb><rt>pʽin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。「<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>Tseo</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>队</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>带掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>醒转来</rb><rt>sing-cün-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>识得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>DÆN-Z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>死人</rb><rt>Si-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>愚笨个</rb><rt>Nyü-beng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>下的个</rb><rt>ʽô-tih-go</rt><rb>种</rb><rt>*cong*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活起来</rb><rt>weh-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>下的个</rb><rt>ʽô-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>将来个</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>镜光</rb><rt>kying-kwông</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>种</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>麦</rb><rt>mah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>别样告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>种</rb><rt>*cong*</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>各样</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt><rb>种</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> ### 1K. XVI.

<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>KÔNG-TAO</rt><rb>周济</rb><rt>tsiu-tsi</rt><rb>圣徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>捐项</rb><rt>kyün-ʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加拉太</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah-tʽa-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>Me</rt><rb>七日个</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>头一日</rb><rt>deo-ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>朵化</rb><rt>tô-hwô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>进业</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抽点出</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tin-cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>积聚间</rb><rt>tsih-jü-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>才方</rb><rt>dzæ-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>捐</rb><rt>kyün</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>举荐个</rb><rt>kyü-tsin-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>捐项</rb><rt>kyün-ʽông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>却勒</rb><rt>kyʽiah-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, eng-we: *kʽen* 2K. 8. 6. 本文字眼，恩惠。看 2K. 8. 6.（原注）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>经过了</rb><rt>kying-ko-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>；（<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马其顿</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>经过个</rb><rt>kying-ko-go</rt></ruby>。）<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>耽搁记</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>冬</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>未可知</rb><rt>vi-kʽo-cü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>相见</rb><rt>siang-kyin</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一落</rb><rt>ih-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>五旬节</rb><rt>Wu-shing-tsih</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一道</rb><rt>ih-dao</rt><rb>广阔</rb><rt>kwông-kwʽeh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>轻</rb><rt>kyʽing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>安安耽耽</rb><rt>en-en-tæn-tæn</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚波罗</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>再三</rb><rt>tsæ-sæn</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>*gyi-go*</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>决</rb><rt>kyüih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>便个</rb><rt>bin-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大丈夫</rb><rt>da-dziang-fu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刚强胆气</rb><rt>kông-gyiang-tæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>仁爱</rb><rt>jing-æ</rt><rb>做主</rb><rt>tso-cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚该亚</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô-go</rt><rb>尝新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>单</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>圣徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby> <sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>劳碌个</rb><rt>lao-loh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>福徒纳</rb><rt>Foh-du-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚该古</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-kwu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>欢喜个</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>弗到之处</rb><rt>feh-tao-ts-cʽü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>补足个</rb><rt>pu-tsoh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>*sing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>安慰过了</rb><rt>en-w̆e-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>认着</rb><rt>nying-djôh</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb><u>亚西亚</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚居</rb><rt>Üô-kyü</rt><rb>拉</rb><rt>lah</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>百基拉</rb><rt>Pah-kyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里个</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>教会</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>切身</rb><rt>tsʽih-sing</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>致致诚诚</rb><rt>ts-ts-dzing-dzing</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>嗅嘴个</rb><rt>hyüong-cü-go</rt><rb>礼</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>亲笔</rb><rt>tsʽing-pih</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>两句</rb><rt>*liang-kyü*</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>灭亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>爱惜心</rb><rt>æ-sih-sing</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
